## F5. Reactors: kinetics and chemical equilibrium

**F5-C1 delivered (Codex, 5 November 2026; independent review pending).** The common Model now declares an optional version-1 equilibrium-reaction record and replays a shared `state_variation` operation from the thermodynamic fundamental law through Gibbs differentials, fixed T/P and a stoichiometric virtual displacement. The compiler checks the selected normalized affinity against that ancestry; a posed final equation or an undone operation is rejected. Conserved-coordinate component balances remain differential and independent reaction extents are algebraic in the version-4 index-one selection. The existing Model panel exposes phase, formation anchor and standard state; no reaction heat is added to formation-referenced chemical potentials.

**F5-C1 independent-review repairs (Codex, 5 November 2026; re-review pending).** F4: Python and TypeScript now require canonical integer tokens in stoichiometric rationals (no `01`, `-01`, `-0` or zero denominator); the Python common reader also catches ArithmeticError and locates the failure at `motores.equilibriumReactions`. F5: index certification failures carry `motores.equilibriumReactions.volumes.<owner>`. F6: a scalar normalization mismatch has `reaction_gas_constant_mismatch`, naming the provider R = 8.314462618 J/(mol K). F3: the virtual displacement has an explicit Model `declare_domain` nonzero premise, retained as an eliminated-coordinate proof condition; the author no longer supplies a virtual-displacement Solve datum.

F2b: inconclusive extent hulls are bisected on the longest relative-width axis, with deterministic tie-breaking and a shared closed split face. Every leaf must have certified provider bounds and nonzero interval Gaussian pivots; the stored split tree proves that the leaves cover the full declared box. Limits are depth 20 and 4,096 evaluated boxes; exhaustion rejects rather than approximating. NH3, N2 + 3 H2 = 2 NH3, n_ref = (1,3,0), 700 K, 100 bar and xi in [0.05,0.9], now certifies with two leaves and determinant enclosure [4.940984940984938, 47.75301891091366]. An uncertified child test rejects the entire box. Pointwise analytic derivatives are additional containment checks, not the proof.

**F1 explicit provider-closure scope:** the recorded Model deduction ends at sum(nu_i mu_i)/(R T) = 0. The constitutive relation mu_i = mu_i^0(T; gas formation anchor) + R T ln(n_i P/(sum(n) P0)) is supplied by the selected public IdealGasPhase provider, not yet a replayed thermodynamic constitutive row in the Model. The declaration names ideal_gas, gas anchor and ideal_gas_P0 and the compiler enforces that provider/reference tuple. This is a provider-backed closure, not a claim that the ideal-gas potential was deduced from conservation. Recording this constitutive row in the Thermodynamics workbench remains a capability extension. **F2 thermal precondition:** the current common volume carries a u_lineal caloric assumption that is inconsistent with the reaction provider caloric potential. It is unused with prescribed T and an inactive energy balance. Thermal coupling must first replace/reconcile that caloric closure with the provider; it remains rejected, and formation energy must still be counted once. Closed-region execution is the verified scope; open and coupled multi-extent execution remain unverified.

Repair checks: 29 focused equilibrium tests and the signed-zero cache regression pass (30 checks across the focused runs); 26 production-client checks and repository TypeScript pass. Cache/template lowering regressions: 7 passed in 279.82 s, including cached/uncached network equality and generic steady specialization. Network/workbench/DAE regressions: 61 passed in 581.26 s. Physical journals: 1,502 checks across 46 models; derivation audit: 133 checks, 46 recorded models. These results close the Codex repair validation run before the subsequent dec-claude-absorbs-codex handoff; they do not certify later changes in Claude worktrees.

**Independent review of F5-C1 (Claude [claude-b], 5 November 2026): approve with repairs.** The admission proof is sound. dg/dxi = (dg/dn) N is composed from the certified `reaction_jacobian_bounds` with exact-rational, outward-rounded binary64 arithmetic, and interval Gaussian elimination with zero-free pivots proves every matrix in the hull nonsingular, so the extent map is injective on the declared box; run-time domain checks keep time, states and extents inside it. 0 containment failures over 1,440 analytic Jacobians in 40 random two-reaction boxes. Compiler states match 50-digit mpmath roots with the provider's K to between 4e-16 and 7e-13 (water-gas shift at 500 and 800 K, a recorded dependent 2nu row, NH3 at 700 K and 1 and 100 bar); against independent chemicals/thermo thermochemistry |d ln K| <= 0.008 and extents differ by <= 0.36 %. Every out-of-scope request was rejected, liquids with a located `PROVIDER_UNSUPPORTED`. Repairs: (F1, medium) the recorded deduction ends at the generic sum nu_i mu_i/(RT) = 0; the ideal-gas mu_i is a provider closure with no recorded thermodynamic relation in the Model; record it in the Thermodynamics panel or state it as a provider closure. (F2, medium) the admitted volume must carry the `u_lineal` caloric assumption, which contradicts the provider; harmless with prescribed T and no energy balance, but it blocks thermal coupling. (F2b, medium) the enclosure goes through the per-species n-box hull and loses n = n_ref + N xi, so NH3 on [0.05, 0.9], positive throughout, is rejected; bisect the extent box or enclose dg/dxi directly. (F3-F6, low) the virtual displacement needs a numeric Solve datum; the stoichiometry `"1/0"` crashes the reader unlocated; certificate paths lack the `motores.` prefix; an R mismatch is reported as `reaction_constraint_not_deduced`. (F7, info) only closed regions are executed; open systems and coupled multi-extent execution are not exercised. Evidence: 20 focused tests (38 s), 59 workbench/F3 regressions (465 s) and 22 client checks pass.

**Re-review of the F5-C1 repairs (Claude [claude-b], 5 November 2026): approve with one repair.** F1: the ideal-gas mu_i is reached through shared recorded operations (`declare_relation` thermodynamics constitutive, `substitute`), and the provider match rejects 17 adversarial variants authored through the same operations (wrong sign, liquid form, wrong R, P0 off by 1e-9, extra terms, swapped or missing species, non-thermodynamic family). F2b: the bisection is a sound whole-box proof of the binary64 box (leaves share faces, exact rational volumes sum to the root, depth 20 and budget 4,096 reject with located messages); 1,050 40-digit Jacobians at every leaf vertex and interior points lie in their leaf enclosures. F2 and F3-F6 hold. Required repair B1-F5-4 (medium): relations are required for every species, but a species with an all-zero stoichiometric column (an inert such as N2) never appears in the affinity, so its relation cannot be substituted and inert-containing models can never be admitted; require relations only for species with a nonzero column, with an inert regression.

**F5-C1 repairs committed (claude-b, 6 November 2026; 7 commits ending 4d39d2a).** B1-F5-4 repaired: recorded chemical-potential relations are required only for species with a nonzero stoichiometric column, so inert species (for example N2 with NH3 synthesis) are admitted; the inert regression matches an independent mpmath root. The F5 test creates `.tmp/` before writing. On fase-a-adopcion: 39 equilibrium tests (277.9 s), 31 client checks and 27 explicit-compiler checks pass. Next: liquid-phase and thermally coupled equilibrium for the reactive stage (B3).

**F5 B3 part 1, liquid-phase equilibrium, committed (claude-b, 6 November 2026; independent review: approve with repairs, R1 and R2 applied in the commit).** `provider: "package"` liquid regions (UNIFAC original and Dortmund, ideal solution) record mu_i = mu_i*(T,P) + R T ln(x_i gamma_i) as a Thermodynamics constitutive row through the shared operations, and the compiler matches it to the executed PackagePhase (R, standard state, functional form, package); the extent proof composes the certified liquid `reaction_jacobian_bounds` with bisection. Liquid esterification extents match an independent thermo UNIFAC plus mpmath root to ~1e-15; inert solvents are admitted; NRTL liquids and package vapour regions are rejected with located `PROVIDER_UNSUPPORTED`; the review authored 14 adversarial relations, all rejected. Contract: optional `packageId` and a 'Liquid package regions' paragraph in `docs/contracts/common-model.md`. On a clean checkout: fast tier green, 54 equilibrium tests (318.8 s) and 31 client checks pass. Part 2 (thermal coupling) is design only: use the package closure as the caloric closure and verify with an adiabatic isobaric (PH) liquid case with dg/dxi|_{H,P} = (dg/dn)N - (dg/dT) Delta_r h^T / C_p (a rigid UV liquid is ill-posed for this incompressible model); it needs version-4 package-closure volumes in `derived.py`, PackageClosure amounts from extents and a C_p enclosure in `jacobian_bounds.py`.

**F5 B3 part 2, thermally coupled equilibrium (claude-b, 6 November 2026; independent review: approve with repairs R1-R3, applied; commits 74e7c15..effd992).** A version-4 selection now admits a package-closure volume that is the region of its own extent block. The region's caloric closure is the reaction package, with the formation reference of the declaration anchor. The closure receives n = n_ref + N xi and returns T = T(H, P, n). The energy row is the recorded closed, prescribed-pressure, adiabatic enthalpy balance with `no_volume_source`, which lowers to dH/dt = 0: the formation-referenced enthalpy carries the reaction heat once, and no source term exists. The index-one proof covers the extent box crossed with a Model-recorded closure-temperature interval, and a runtime check (`reaction_temperature_out_of_interval`) keeps T inside it. On that box the proof composes dg/dxi|_{H,P} = (dg/dn)N + R T^2 (dg/dT)(dg/dT)^T / C_p. A new certified `heat_capacity_bounds` (C_p = -T d2G/dT2, interval AD in T, UNIFAC excess) proves C_p > 0 on every leaf, and bisection runs over extents and T.

An adiabatic isobaric UNIFAC liquid esterification (feed 340 K) reaches xi = 0.6340065654906744 and T = 350.0730196113591 K, within 1.6e-15 mol and 4.4e-11 K of an independent mpmath/thermo solution. A package-vapour ideal-gas case (2 ethanol = 1-butanol + water with toluene, 20 kPa, feed 360 K) reaches T = 408.447926152 K, within 3e-13 K of a 50-digit solution, with xi within 1e-16. Water-gas shift is not representable in a package vapour, because the catalog has no CO, CO2 or H2 pure data. Energy is conserved to 1e-10 against the independent enthalpy. The independent review's own mpmath reference (its own UNIFAC and excess enthalpy) matched five coupled cases to about 1e-14 in extent and 1e-11 K.

Located rejections cover: u_lineal and missing formation references (lost heat); a volumetric source (double counting); a foreign closure package; a rigid liquid (ill-posed, Delta_r v = +0.35 cm3/mol); a missing or exceeded T interval; and temperature-datum initialization. 16 new tests all fail on the previous code. The liquid, constraint, repair and Jacobian-bound suites and the fast tier stay green.

Near-complete conversion needs a Newton step tolerance of 1e-15 (1e-12 stagnates), recorded as a follow-up for `core/solvers/nonlinear.py`. Pending: feed-temperature initialization, rigid coupled regions, and open and multi-extent coupled execution.

The compiler calls only public `chemical_equilibrium` APIs. It constructs the entire amount box from `n_ref + N xi`, with exact-rational operations rounded outward to binary64, composes `(dg/dn) N`, and proves nonsingularity by interval Gaussian elimination with nonzero interval pivots. Only `certified: true` provider bounds admit the block. Exact stoichiometric coefficients that cannot be transported exactly to the binary64 public API are rejected. Dependent reaction rows remain fresh chemical-certificate checks. Point Jacobians drive Newton only; agreement with `solve_extents` and `minimise_gibbs` does not establish the proof. Existing DAE proof/diagnostic fields publish the determinant enclosure and provider/reference fingerprints; the full composed proof and fresh chemical certificate are retained internally, without an unversioned Results extension.

**Verified compiler scope:** one IdealGasPhase reaction region, prescribed positive T/P, declared positive amount/extent boxes, conserved-coordinate initialization and no active coupled energy balance. PackagePhase requests return located `PROVIDER_UNSUPPORTED`, even though Claude has since extended the public bounds provider to liquid packages. Liquid integration, coupled thermal/network equilibrium and steady selection composition remain pending; no provider downgrade, approximation or earlier-model fallback is permitted. Changes to the declaration or its pinned source operations invalidate the formulation and results.

**Evidence:** `tests/test_equilibrium_reaction_constraint.py`: 20 passed (40.84 s), covering recorded water-gas-shift BDF execution against both independent public equilibrium solvers, one- and two-extent enclosures, dependent rows, exact endpoint rounding and located rejection cases. The combined F5/workbench/F3 index-one regression run passed 68 tests (572.04 s); the final focused run includes subsequent rejection additions. `frontend/verificar_equilibrium_reactions.mjs`: 22 production-reader and shared-presentation checks. Repository TypeScript check passed. Physical journals: 1,502 checks / 46 models; derivation audit: 133 checks / 46 recorded models, no review gaps (structural evidence, not a mathematical certificate). No changes to `core/reactions/` or `core/termodinamica/` were required.


**Kinetic increment, 30 October 2026; implemented, independent review pending.** Primary workspace: Model. Reactions, assumptions, reference, volume and units produce replayable equations for Transform → Solve → Results. Physics/reference edits invalidate dependent formulations/runs; labels do not. This user-authorized slice reuses accepted F3 functionality without depending on completion of broader F3; the board therefore retains delivered F2 as its phase dependency. Chemical-equilibrium constraints wait for Claude API agreement. Rescued tools/seven tests remain separate reference evidence.

**Balance deduction.** Start from local component mass conservation; declare constant `M_i`, `m_i=M_i n_i`, and stoichiometric definition `s_i=M_i sum_j nu_ij r_j`, separately from kinetic constitutive laws. Record substitution, integration, Gauss/Reynolds, inventories, ports and uniform rates to obtain `dn_i/dt=F_i+sum_j nu_ij r_j V`. Here `nu` is species × reactions, products positive; net `r` is mol/(m3 s), and `V` is the reacting volume (phase volume for phase-specific rates). Integrated mol/s rates omit V. Pin input IDs, typed before/after expressions, conditions, units and Undo/Redo. Check elements/charge and `sum_i M_i nu_ij=0`; derive dependent total mass by summing components. Moles need not be conserved. Missing operations require shared-tool extension/replay.

**Chemical energy once.** With formation-consistent package storage/stream energies, retain total `U/H` and add **no reaction-heat source**. Chain-rule differentiation plus component substitution exposes `-sum_j(sum_i nu_ij hbar_i) r_j V` in the temperature equation under declared enthalpy assumptions; rigid-volume reduction uses internal-energy derivatives. Sensible energy requires recorded reference subtraction in both storage and streams. Reject missing/mixed references; never append the rescued heat helper to total-energy RHS.

**Equilibrium versus kinetics.** Kinetics supplies unit-bearing forward/reverse rate laws, with detailed-balance compatibility where applicable. Equilibrium instead imposes `g_j=sum_i nu_ij ln(a_i)-ln(K_j(T))=nu_j^T mu/(RT)=0`. Record independent reaction columns and Transform's full left-nullspace coordinates `b=L n; n=N b+nu_e xi`, with `L nu_e=0, L N=I`. Integrate `bdot=L(F+nu_k r_k V)` and energy; solve extents `xi` algebraically. Independent species integration plus equilibrium constraints is not index one. Reuse F3 nested Newton/BDF, fixed differential initialization and original residual/drift/checked-prefix publication; reject separately fixed off-equilibrium species data. Require nonsingular algebraic Jacobian including thermal closure coupling. Begin in a smooth positive single-phase domain. F3 only admits rational arithmetic: logarithms/property calls, derivatives and rigorous domain/index enclosures require extensions. Simultaneous chemical/phase equilibrium remains pending; depletion/singular/domain failures must be explicit.

**Implemented kinetic subset.** Common-model-backed documents declare `motores.kineticReactions` v1: explicit molar-mass data, elemental/charge rows, rational stoichiometry, nonnegative forward concentration laws with integer orders 0..6, and optional Arrhenius dependence. Reverse laws are separate columns. A fixed positive reacting volume must agree with the control-volume geometry. The existing fundamental material producer, Gauss/Reynolds, uniform-source reductions and total-mass sum are reused; source definitions enter the selected component balances through pinned substitution/expansion/isolation operations. Missing source ancestry, conflicting data/closures, stale operations and unsupported formulation versions reject. Nonnegative amounts and caloric/temperature domains are enforced; the old generated/structural model cannot be substituted.

Implementation follows the proposal's boundaries: `core/modelo/reaction_definition.py` and the common readers validate declarations; `core/balances/reaction_equations.py` supplies definition/constitutive roots and explicit elemental/mass conditions; `material_derivation.py` exposes individual source-retention/uniformity operations; `equation_workbench.py` merges these roots into the existing family graph. `core/compilador/derived.py` evaluates the same declarations in identity derived execution v1 through common RK45/BDF; SI-bound parameters retain dimensions in reconstructed inventories such as sum(M_i*n_i). No reaction integrator or balance template was introduced. Frontend `ReactionControls.tsx`, `kineticReactions.ts` and existing workbench/common-model readers extend the same assumptions panel; `ModelWorksheet.tsx` is unchanged. Shared equation Undo/Redo and stale-source recovery retain the actual saved operations.

The adiabatic reference declares its single-phase molar caloric/formation relation in Thermodynamics and integrates the original total internal-energy law with no added reaction heat. Real-package/UNIFAC activities, phase-specific rates, other rate bases, steady/DAE reaction execution, sensible-energy transformations and chemical-equilibrium constraints remain pending. Neither this caloric assumption nor numerical convergence is a thermodynamic package certificate.

**Proposed Claude facade; agree signatures before implementation, preserving existing APIs:**

```python
evaluate_reactions(provider, nu, *, T, P, n, phase_id, standard_state) -> ReactionState
kinetic_rates(laws, state, *, rate_basis) -> Rates
reaction_jacobian_bounds(provider, nu, box, *, phase_id, standard_state) -> JacobianBounds
certify_reactions(state, *, tolerances) -> Certificate
```

Agree SI, ordering, signs and reference/package fingerprints. State returns ln(a), ln(K), mu, partial molar h/u, derivatives and validity; never equilibrate supplied inventories or clip activities. Bounds support coupled index admission. Certification separates mu residuals/conservation/stability from convergence.

**API differences confirmed by Codex, 2 November 2026 (original handoff by Claude, 30 October 2026).** `evaluate_reactions` and `certify_reactions` exist in `core/reactions/chemical_equilibrium.py` with the proposed keywords and no clipping; the differences are thermodynamic. (1) `provider` is a phase object (`PackagePhase(sistema, tipo, anchor=...)` or `IdealGasPhase`) that fixes the phase and the formation anchor; `phase_id` and `standard_state` (`ideal_gas_P0` | `pure_liquid_TP`) are checked against it, because the standard state belongs to the phase and the anchor, not to a free label. (2) `certify_reactions(state, *, tolerances, n0=None, A=None)` needs the initial inventory and the element matrix to certify conservation; it recomputes mu from the provider and never trusts the state's numbers. (3) The state's mu, h_i and u_i include the formation reference; an energy balance that uses them must not add a heat of reaction (consistent with "Chemical energy once"). (4) `dmu_dn` and `dmu/dT` are central differences, not analytic. `kinetic_rates` and `reaction_jacobian_bounds` are not delivered.

**`reaction_jacobian_bounds` delivered (Claude [claude-b], 5 November 2026; independent review pending).** `core/reactions/jacobian_bounds.py`, re-exported as `core.reactions.chemical_equilibrium.reaction_jacobian_bounds`, implements the requested contract `reaction_jacobian_bounds(provider, nu, box, *, phase_id, standard_state) -> dict` with `format` `rage.reaction-jacobian-bounds`, `version` 1, `certified`, `species`, `nu` (reaction rows, provider order, products positive), `phase_id`, `standard_state`, `anchor`, `box`, `fingerprints` (`package`, `pureData`, `formationReference`, SHA-256 of the provider model, the Poling cp/R coefficients and ranges, and Hf/S0 with their provenance), `bounds`, `proof` and located `diagnostics`. Certified `bounds` hold binary64 pairs for `g` = nu mu/(RT), `dg_dn` [1/mol], `dg_dT` [1/K] and `dg_dP` [1/Pa] that enclose the provider's mathematical function over the whole box. First scope: `IdealGasPhase`, where g = A(T) + sum nu_i ln x_i + Dnu ln(P/P0), dg/dT = -(R DHp(T) + DHf)/(R T^2) (Gibbs-Helmholtz, so the 1/(RT) derivative is included and the formation reference is counted once, never as an added reaction heat), dg/dn_k = nu_k/n_k - Dnu/N and dg/dP = Dnu/P. Method: mpmath interval arithmetic at 113 bits with directed rounding; on 64 equal T pieces the natural extension is intersected with the mean-value form; exact monotone ranges for x_i, 1/n_k, 1/N and the P terms; endpoints rounded floor/ceiling to binary64 and widened by one more ulp. Validity proof, each item checked: provider type with matching phase and standard state, T box inside every species' Poling cp range, strictly positive T/P/n lower bounds, and exact rational element balance of every row (the condition under which absolute entropies replace formation entropies). Malformed input raises `ChemicalEquilibriumError` with a code and `detail['path']` (`PROVIDER_TYPE`, `PHASE_MISMATCH`, `STANDARD_STATE_MISMATCH`, `NU_SHAPE`, `NU_NONFINITE`, `BOX_KEYS`, `BOX_SHAPE`, `BOX_TYPE`, `BOX_NONFINITE`, `BOX_UNORDERED`, `BOX_NONPOSITIVE`). Uncertifiable cases return `certified: false`, null bounds/proof and diagnostics `PROVIDER_UNSUPPORTED` (every `PackagePhase`; no ideal-gas or sampled substitute), `OUT_OF_DOMAIN` (`box.T`), `FORMATION_REFERENCE_INVALID` (`nu[j]`), `ELEMENTS_UNAVAILABLE` or `NONFINITE_BOUND` (for example an underflowing trace, `bounds.dg_dn[j][k]`). The enclosure is of the mathematical provider function with its binary64 data taken as exact; the provider's own float evaluation is a point estimate (at NH3 synthesis, 700 K, it is 1.9e-14 below the 50-digit value, which the singleton enclosure contains). **Verification:** `tests/test_reaction_jacobian_bounds.py` passes 19 tests (115.6 s) against references that never reuse the module's closed forms: g rebuilt in mpmath by quadrature of cp and cp/T, derivatives by 40-digit-plus central `mp.diff`; ammonia point and box (all 32 corners plus interior points), hand-written rational dg/dn and dg/dP, methanol synthesis plus water-gas shift (a Dnu = 0 row) with 3,128 provider samples (3,000 random plus all 128 corners) inside the g enclosure and the provider's finite-difference dg/dn inside within its noise allowance, -R T^2 dg/dT against sum nu_i h_i of `evaluate_reactions` (1e-6 relative), directed and widened conversion, strict containment of singleton boxes, trace inventories (1e-14 to 1e-12 mol certified and enclosing; 1e-320 located `NONFINITE_BOUND`), every rejection code with its path, fingerprint stability and sensitivity to the formation source, and a narrow-box tightness check. `tests/test_chemical_equilibrium.py` (15), `tests/test_reaction_relations.py` (7) and `tests/test_kinetic_reactions.py` (15) still pass. Not delivered: `kinetic_rates` and the F5-C1 compiler composition dg/dxi = (dg/dn) N with its nonsingularity proof, which belongs to Codex.

**`reaction_jacobian_bounds` extended to package phases (Claude [claude-b], 5 November 2026; independent review pending).** The same call, output (`format` `rage.reaction-jacobian-bounds`, `version` 1), validity proof and located diagnostics now certify `PackagePhase` providers: a UNIFAC (original, Dortmund, LLE) or ideal liquid with standard state `pure_liquid_TP`, and the package's ideal vapour with `ideal_gas_P0`. Liquid: g = sum nu_i phi_i(T)/(RT) + P sum nu_i v_L,i/(RT) + sum nu_i (ln x_i + ln gamma_i), where phi_i is the package's pure-liquid Gibbs energy without its v_L P term plus the anchor's Hf_i - T S0_i (Psat route, or the tabulated cp_L route of a liquid-only species); hence dg/dP = sum nu_i v_L,i/(RT) (incompressible liquids, no excess volume) and dg/dT includes the derivative of 1/(RT) and counts the formation reference once. New files (Claude-owned, no existing thermodynamic file changed): `core/termodinamica/interval_ad.py`, sparse second-order forward automatic differentiation over mpmath intervals, and `core/termodinamica/unifac_intervals.py`, UNIFAC ln gamma in amount form built from the model's `identidad()` parameter set with analytic first and second derivatives (r_i, q_i and r_i^(3/4) recomputed in intervals). Method: the standard part by interval AD in T on the 64 T pieces (natural extension intersected with the mean-value form); the composition part by interval AD in (T, n) on at most 64 sub-boxes, split along the largest |dC/dv| width estimates, intersecting the natural and mean-value forms (Hessian enclosure for the gradient) of the excess plus the exact mixing term and of the whole composition term; hull returned. Validity adds a supported liquid model (NRTL is not extended: `PROVIDER_UNSUPPORTED` at `provider.sistema.liq`), T inside `Sistema.T_rango` and P inside [P_MIN, P_MAX] (`OUT_OF_DOMAIN` at `box.T` or `box.P`), the liquid-only admission rule rechecked (`LIQUID_ANCHOR_REQUIRED` at `provider.anchor`), and `ENCLOSURE_DOMAIN` if an interval argument leaves a function's domain. Package results add the fingerprint `activityModel` (SHA-256 of `identidad()`: variant, table hashes, assignments, R/Q and used interactions; or the ideal model), and the package fingerprint now also pins the package version, liquid model, T and P ranges, constants and substitutions; `proof.excess_pieces` records the sub-box split. Tightness (Dortmund esterification, enclosure width over the sampled range): g 1.02, 1.12 and 2.2 for amount boxes of +-0.1 %, +-1 % and +-10 %; dg/dn 1.03-1.08, 1.2-1.4 and 3-5.6. A four-species UNIFAC box takes about 4 s. **Verification:** `tests/test_reaction_jacobian_bounds_liquid.py` passes 31 tests (66.7 s) against references that never reuse production code: UNIFAC rebuilt in 50-digit mpmath from thermo's DOUFSG/DOUFIP2016 and UFSG/UFIP tables and DDBST assignments in mole-fraction form; pure liquids from chemicals' Wagner, Poling cp and Perry cp_L rows by quadrature; d ln gamma/dn and d ln gamma/dT by complex step (step 1e-30), dg/dT and dg/dP by central mp.diff. Cases: interval-AD rules against mp.diff; interval UNIFAC value and gradient at points (esterification with Dortmund and original, ethanol/1-butanol at 1e-6 mol/water) containing the complex-step references, whose Gibbs-Duhem sums are below 1e-40; ethanol + acetic acid = ethyl acetate + water (acetic acid liquid only, liquid anchor) with Dortmund, original and ideal liquid: strict containment of the reference by point boxes (the provider's float value lies about 4e-14 from it, some 60 times the enclosure width), box corners and interior points, 1,564 provider samples (1,500 random plus all 64 corners) inside the g enclosure and the provider's finite-difference dg/dn inside within its noise, 0 in sum_k [n_k][dg/dn_k] over the box and at a point (width below 1e-13), -R T^2 dg/dT against sum nu_i h_i (1e-5), and tightness at +-0.1 %; Guerbet 2 ethanol = 1-butanol + water in the package vapour (Dnu = 0) and in the UNIFAC liquid with 1e-9 mol butanol; rejections (T past acetic acid's cp_L range, P above 5e5 Pa, a tampered anchor, an unbalanced row, NRTL, a standard-state mismatch); fingerprint stability and sensitivity; the liquid anchor reproducing chemicals' Hfl - T0 S0l at T0 (1e-9). `tests/test_reaction_jacobian_bounds.py` (19; its package test now targets NRTL), `tests/test_chemical_equilibrium.py` (15), `tests/test_reaction_relations.py` (7), `tests/test_kinetic_reactions.py` (15) and the UNIFAC/thermodynamics regressions still pass. For F5-C1 with a liquid: build `PackagePhase(Sistema(package, cas, solo_liquido=True), 'liquido', anchor='liquid')` when acetic acid is present, call with `phase_id='liquido'`, `standard_state='pure_liquid_TP'`, admit only `certified: true`, and keep the amount box narrow (Krawczyk-sized). Not delivered: NRTL phases and a bound on the package's phase stability, which this enclosure does not assess.

**Kinetic verification.** `tests/test_kinetic_reactions.py` passes 14 tests using fresh shared-law authors, never product-library examples: analytic isothermal A -> B under RK45/BDF (maximum amount errors 1.46e-10/5.37e-9 mol), adiabatic T=300+100*(1-exp(-0.1*t)) K (maximum error 1.31e-8 K; T(40 s)=398.1684361 K), an independent DOP853 Arrhenius/temperature reduction with tenfold refinement, forward/reverse superposition and A -> 2B with changing total moles but conserved mass. Tests also cover elemental/charge/mass/unit errors, actual before/after replay, dependent totals, labels, source-substitution bypass, missing caloric closure, fixed-binding conflicts, negative initialization, Undo/Redo and stale-source recovery. `frontend/verificar_kinetic_reactions.mjs` consumes fresh Python publications through production readers and renders the shared controls. All 1,391 physical-journal/layout checks and 55 physical derivation tests pass. Frozen M1 U1 passes all 12 reference/refinement trajectories (largest scaled error 3.79924e-9); all 12 coupled deduction/independent-reference tests also pass (1439.254 s). Input hashes and 43/31/25 operation counts remain unchanged. All 40 F3 affine, nonlinear, index-one DAE and steady regressions pass (1539.906 s), together with 17 generic Newton and 12 physical equation graph tests; no general equilibrium integration is claimed. Standard TypeScript sees the existing 16 stale generated .next references to removed routes; repository-source TypeScript excluding generated caches and scoped ESLint pass. The existing English capability registry and canonical snapshot are updated. Remaining acceptance includes the Claude API, thermodynamic/activity integration and the equilibrium extent/Gibbs comparison.

**Kinetic zero-count repair (Codex, 30 October 2026): repaired, re-review pending.** Commit `453bdf6` preserves the 14 F5 kinetics source/test files through explicit file-by-file staging and `git commit --only`; shared documents and unrelated work remain outside that commit. Elemental/charge declarations now omit zero-count terms instead of constructing unevaluated `Mul(0, nu)` products, avoiding intermittent SymPy `InconsistentAssumptions`. The new zero-count regression and the other 14 kinetic tests pass in 20 fresh processes with `PYTHONHASHSEED=1..20` (300 test executions); seeds 2..20 retain logs under ignored `.tmp/f5-zero-count-seeds/`. The frontend kinetics publication/reader/control verifier also passes. This repairs construction without extending reaction physics; independent kinetic re-review remains required. Port 8005 was not used.

**Independent review of the kinetic increment (Claude, 30 October 2026): one finding, not accepted yet.** Deduction: the component laws reach `dn_i/dt=sum_j nu_ij r_j V` through pinned substitute (`m_i=M_i n_i`, generation/consumption definitions), expand and isolate on the fundamental material root, with real before/after receipts; rate laws are separate constitutive nodes; the total is the dependent component sum. Energy: the adiabatic case integrates `dU/dt=0` with formation energy inside the caloric `U`; no reaction-heat term exists in the compiler or energy journal. Own probe `python .tmp/claude-f5-kinetics-review/probe.py` (fresh N-species shared-law authors; `results.json`), worst errors: isothermal A -> B against `2 exp(-0.1 t)` 1.9e-11 (RK45) / 8.2e-10 mol (BDF); hand Arrhenius k(350 K)=1.75249836684 1/s, relative 6.0e-11; adiabatic with species-dependent cv (100/150 J/(mol K)), u_f,B=-12 kJ/mol and Arrhenius coupling, T against U=U0 algebra on an independent DOP853 trajectory 3.3e-9 K, end 379.38333 K equal to the complete-conversion energy balance, U drift 0; A+B -> C, A+C -> D with an inert, mixed-sign C and zero initial C/D, 8.5e-12 mol against DOP853, mass drift 1.4e-17 kg, element drift 8.9e-16 mol, inert unchanged. Zero-order depletion stops with an explicit `agotamiento` event at t=40 s; per-mass rate units, wrong order units, mass/element violations and an inert-only reaction reject structurally. `tests/test_kinetic_reactions.py` 14 passed (47.8 s); the four F3 files 40 passed (1167.5 s). **Finding (repair required):** `core/balances/reaction_equations.py` builds the elemental root from `sp.Mul(Integer(count), nu, evaluate=False)`; when a species lacks an element (count 0, nearly every real reaction), SymPy 1.14 `Mul(0, -1, evaluate=False).is_real` raises `InconsistentAssumptions` in about 3-5 % of processes (hash-order dependent), from `validity.finite` during `graph.add`, so authoring or lowering fails unstructured for the whole process. Reproducers: `python .tmp/claude-f5-kinetics-review/sympy_zero_mul.py` under `PYTHONHASHSEED` 0..40, and `bimolecular_flaky.py` (A+B -> C) in a loop: 2 of 30 runs fail (`flaky-runs.log`). The delivered tests miss it because A -> B has no zero element count. Keep the premise typed without unevaluated zero products (for example, omit zero-count terms or keep the coefficient pairs as data) and add a zero-count regression run under several hash seeds. No production, test, board or Checklist file was edited.

**Kinetic re-review after the zero-count repair (Claude, 30 October 2026): accepted.** Commit `453bdf6` keeps only nonzero elemental/charge products in `reaction_equations.py`; an all-zero row becomes the typed root 0 = 0 (regression `test_zero_element_counts_omit_unevaluated_products`). Rerun of the earlier reproducers in `.tmp/claude-f5-kinetics-review/`: `bimolecular_flaky.py` (A+B -> C, species with zero element counts) solves in 30 of 30 fresh processes (`rereview-flaky-runs.log`; before the repair 2 of 30 failed); `nonzero_terms.py`, the same assumption query on the surviving term kinds (unit, signed charge, rational nu), passes under `PYTHONHASHSEED` 0..80; the raw SymPy `Mul(0, -1)` probe still fails in 2 of 41 seeds, confirming the repair avoids the construction rather than a lucky seed. `tests/test_kinetic_reactions.py` passes 15 of 15 under `PYTHONHASHSEED` 1, 3, 4, 19, 101 and 202 (`rereview-tests-seed*.log`). `probe.py` reproduces every earlier reference error unchanged (worst 3.3e-9 K adiabatic, 8.2e-10 mol BDF). Acceptance covers the kinetic subset described above; equilibrium constraints, real-package activities and other rate bases remain pending.

**Chemical equilibrium on the package potentials (Claude, 30 October 2026; implemented; independent review accepted after two repairs — see below).** `core/reactions/chemical_equilibrium.py` takes mu_i from `core.mezclas.Sistema.mu` (UNIFAC or ideal liquid, ideal gas) plus a constant formation shift Hf_i - T S0_i per species (`chemicals.reaction` ideal-gas Hfg/S0g, source selectable). K(T) therefore carries the package's own cp integrals; the constant-dH van 't Hoff value is reported beside it. Species outside the catalog or above its liquid range use an ideal-gas phase built from the same `Especie.g_ig0` and Poling cp. The module offers: stoichiometric rank with an independent subset and the dependent combinations; element conservation; the extent formulation over the independent reactions; Gibbs minimisation over ln n with element or reaction-invariant constraints, polished on its own stationarity conditions; and a certificate recomputed from fresh mu (max |sum nu mu|/RT < 1e-9, conservation, n > 0, package tangent-plane stability). `tests/test_chemical_equilibrium.py` passes 11 tests: (a) NH3 log10 Kf from JANAF data 500 K -0.50126 vs -0.501 and 700 K -2.02894 vs -2.029 (NIST-JANAF H-083); the default ATcT Hf (-45.558 vs -45.898 kJ/mol) moves it 0.036, a data-source difference. The extent matches the closed form within 1e-11. (b) Ethanol + acetic acid, UNIFAC Dortmund, liquid anchor, 298.15 K, equimolar feed: conversion 0.6718 vs about 2/3 (Berthelot and Pean de Saint-Gilles, 1862); original UNIFAC gives 0.6466. ln K equals the tabulated liquid Hfl/S0l value within 1e-10. (c) CO/CO2/H2/H2O/MeOH at 500 K and 5 MPa: extent and Gibbs minimisation agree within 9e-16 mol. (d) Three reactions of rank 2: the dependent ln K matches within 1.3e-14 and all three residuals are certified. Unbalanced reactions are rejected. `tests/test_reaction_relations.py` (7 tests) and the catalog consumers stay green.

**Findings (30 October 2026).** (F5-T1) The package's pure liquid (ideal gas + Psat) misplaces a vapour-dimerising acid: acetic acid g_L sits 4.9 kJ/mol above the liquid formation data (dGf,l -383.8 vs CRC -389.9 kJ/mol), so the gas anchor gives a conversion of 0.868. `anchor="liquid"` shifts each species to its tabulated Hfl/S0l at T0. For the existing catalog species the two anchors differ by 0.03-0.94 kJ/mol. (F5-T2) Acetic acid is NOT in the published catalog. Registering it would admit it to the VLE packages, whose ideal vapour is invalid for it; it needs a liquid-only admission rule first (a catalog/admission decision, with Codex for compiler admission). The test registers it in-process only, with its pure data checked: Psat -1.8 % at 298 K and -0.09 % at 391 K vs NIST Antoine; cp_ig 63.35 vs CRC 63.4 J/(mol K); v_L density -0.3 %; DDBST CH3 + COOH. (F5-T3) A single-phase chemical equilibrium can be phase-unstable (butanol-water split; vapour at 373 K and 1 bar). The certificate reports this; simultaneous chemical and phase equilibrium remains open. (F5-C1, Codex) Model/compiler need: an equilibrium-reaction constraint g_j = nu_j^T mu/RT = 0 calling `evaluate_reactions`, the anchor/standard-state choice exposed as a recorded assumption, and admission of the extents as unknowns.

**Independent review of the chemical-equilibrium module (Claude, 30 October 2026): mathematics accepted, two repairs required before commit.** `core/catalogo.py` is unchanged from HEAD. Accepted: mu_i = `Sistema.mu` + Hf_i - T S0_i is a constant per-species shift, so K(T) = exp(-sum nu mu_i^0/RT) carries the package's own Kirchhoff and dCp/T integrals; `g_ig0` is the pure ideal gas at (T, P0 = 1 bar), the JANAF convention; `g_L = h_L - T s_L` holds identically in `core/mezclas_datos.py`, so the liquid anchor reproduces Hfl - T0 S0l exactly; the liquid standard state is the pure liquid at (T, P) and K there is pressure-dependent by declaration; activities are the package's UNIFAC ln x + ln gamma with no separate gamma path; `certify` recomputes mu and checks every declared reaction, including dependent ones. The 11 tests pass (1.7 s). Independent checks (`python .tmp/claude-f5-equilibrium-review/counter_check.py`): water-gas shift K 136.2 / 28.05 / 4.183 / 1.423 at 500 / 600 / 800 / 1000 K against Moe (1962) 124.7 / 27.10 / 4.024 / 1.281; an own Kirchhoff integration with a different cp source (`thermo.HeatCapacityGas`) differs by |ln K| <= 1.2e-2 up to 1000 K (cp-source spread); methanol synthesis K 2.08e-2 / 1.95e-3 bar^-2 at 473 / 523 K against the Graaf (1986) fit 1.74e-2 / 1.59e-3 (ln K +0.18, a known fit-versus-table spread, not attributed to the module); NH3 at 400 K and 10 MPa, extent and Gibbs agree to 1e-8 mol. **(F5-EQ-1, repair)** `solve_extents` stalls near a vanishing reactant: H2 + 1/2 O2 = H2O at 600 K, 1 bar, feed (2, 1, 0), ln K = 42.89, returns n_H2 = 1.35e-8 with residual 14.3 (closed form 9.63e-13); `minimise_gibbs` over ln n gives 9.631255e-13 and certifies. The certificate reports `certified=False`, so nothing false is accepted, but the extent route fails silently for strongly product-favoured reactions: the constant penalty for n <= 0 gives Levenberg-Marquardt a zero Jacobian. Bound the step to the positive domain (fraction-to-boundary) or solve in ln n, and raise a structured error when the final certificate fails. **(F5-EQ-2, repair)** `evaluate_reactions` returns `valid=True` with NaN in `dmu_dn` whenever an amount is below the fixed central step 1e-7 n_total (`python .tmp/claude-f5-equilibrium-review/trace_derivatives.py`: NH3 1e-8 of 4 mol gives dmu_dn[NH3,NH3] = NaN, exact 5.82e11 J/mol^2). F5-C1 would feed this Jacobian to Newton. Scale the step per species (or use the analytic ideal-gas term RT(delta_ik/n_i - 1/N) plus the package's ln gamma derivative) and mark the state invalid if any derivative is not finite. **(Low)** A species absent from the feed and untouched by every reaction makes `solve_extents` raise `NO_INTERIOR` with a misleading message, while `minimise_gibbs` returns a phantom 1.6e-18 mol and certifies; detect such species and exclude or report them. No production, test or Checklist file was edited. **Repaired; re-review accepted (Claude, 30 October 2026).** (F5-EQ-1) `solve_extents` now runs a damped Newton in z = ln n on the independent-reaction affinities plus the reaction invariants B (n - n0) = 0, with at most a factor e^5 per step and backtracking: amounts are carried directly, so a nearly exhausted reactant keeps its relative precision and every iterate is positive; the extents are recovered from n - n0. A final state that fails the recomputed certificate raises `NOT_CERTIFIED` with the certificate attached. The H2 + 1/2 O2 case gives n_H2 = 9.6324e-13 against the bisection 9.6313e-13 and Gibbs 9.6313e-13 (relative 1.2e-4, i.e. 1.1e-16 mol: n_H2O = 2 fixes n_H2 only to double precision of the H balance), certified. (F5-EQ-2) `dmu_dn` uses a central step 1e-6 n_k per species, and for a trace species (n_k < 1e-3 n_total) the other rows take a three-point forward step 1e-5 n_total; a non-finite derivative marks the state invalid. NH3 1e-8 of 4 mol: dmu_dn[NH3,NH3] relative 1.7e-9 against RT(1/n - 1/N), all entries within 1e-6. (Low) Species with zero feed that no reaction touches raise `ABSENT_INERT_SPECIES` in both solvers. Regressions in `NearBoundary` of `tests/test_chemical_equilibrium.py`: 15 tests pass; `tests/test_reaction_relations.py` 7 pass; `core/catalogo.py` unchanged. **Re-review verdict: accepted.** The reviewer re-read the solver and derivative code and reran its own reproducers: `counter_check.py` check 4 now gives extent n_H2 = 9.6325e-13 (closed form 9.6313e-13), certified; NH3 at 400 K and 10 MPa still agrees between extent and Gibbs; an absent inert raises `ABSENT_INERT_SPECIES` in both solvers; `trace_derivatives.py` gives a finite dmu_dn[NH3,NH3] = 5.8201238e11 against exact 5.8201238e11 J/mol^2 with no NaN. `tests/test_chemical_equilibrium.py` 15 pass (2.0 s); `core/catalogo.py` shows no diff from HEAD. Note, not a defect: the extent and Gibbs splits of the two trace reactants differ by about 1e-16 mol, the double-precision limit of the H and O balances beside n_H2O = 2.

**Demo 0.1 batch reactor feasibility audit (Claude, 30 October 2026).** Target: a non-isothermal liquid batch reactor deduced from component mass and energy conservation. Read against the uncommitted kinetic slice.

- **Expressible now.** The stoichiometric source reaches `dn_i/dt=sum_j nu_ij r_j V` through the pinned substitute, expand and isolate operations. The forward power-law rate with Arrhenius is a separate constitutive node. Jacket heat also works with the shared operations. The energy root `dU/dt=energy_heat_rate` takes a declared constitutive relation `Q=UA(T_j-T)` by `substitute`, giving `dU/dt=UA(T_j-T)`. Temperature is reconstructed from the authored caloric relation. Probe `.tmp/claude-demo-reactor/jacket_probe.py` (from the repository root, `PYTHONPATH="tests;."`): A -> B, E=50 kJ/mol, Cv 100 J/(mol K), u_f,B=-10 kJ/mol, UA 20 W/K, T_j 300 K, identity derived execution RK45. Against an independent DOP853 integration, n_A differs by at most 1.6e-10 mol and U by 3.8e-6 J. T peaks at 388.3 K and ends at 302.18 K.
- **Blocker 1 (Codex), cleared.** The kinetic slice and its zero-element-term repair are committed in 453bdf6. The independent re-review is pending.
- **Blocker 2, F5-C2 (Codex, Claude API).** Reaction enthalpy from formation data cannot come from the common thermodynamic layer. Derived execution lowers only authored caloric relations with numeric constants (`u_lineal`, or a declared relation such as the kinetic test's `formationB`); it never calls `core.mezclas`. Copying Hf values in as data would bypass the common layer. Codex must add a Thermodynamics relation node: caloric inventory `U=sum_i n_i u_i(T,P)` from a named package and formation anchor, pinned by its package/reference fingerprint. Its lowering must evaluate u_i and dU/dT for the temperature reconstruction. Claude supplies the values from `evaluate_reactions(...)` (u_i and h_i already include the formation reference), or a narrower caloric facade agreed with Codex. With that node, the reaction heat appears through chain-rule differentiation, as in "Chemical energy once", with no added source term. Claude API delivered (30 October 2026): `phase_caloric(..., anchor='liquid', liquid_only=True)` gives `U`, `dU_dT` and `u_partial` for the esterification liquid, `phase_temperature` reconstructs T from U with a stability check, and `reaction_enthalpy` reports Delta_r H for Results; Codex only has to call them from derived execution. Exact contract under the [F4 flash drum audit](#f4-boundaries-and-real-equipment).
- **Blocker 3, F5-C3 (Codex).** The equilibrium-limit check is not expressible. A reverse law takes its own independent constant, with no detailed-balance relation `k_r=k_f/K_c(T)` using K from `evaluate_reactions`. Results also has no row that runs `certify_reactions` on the final state. The minimum is that Results verification row, comparing the long-time kinetic state with the certified equilibrium at the same T and inventory. The detailed-balance relation is the full repair.
- **Blocker 4, F5-T2 (decided by the user on 30 October 2026: use the esterification and admit acetic acid as liquid-only; Claude side delivered, see below).** The catalog has eight species: water, ethanol, benzene, toluene, 1-butanol, ethyl acetate, methanol and acetone. None of them form a liquid reaction with published kinetics on their own. Recommended system: ethanol + acetic acid = ethyl acetate + water. Its equilibrium is already validated (`tests/test_chemical_equilibrium.py`, conversion 0.672 against about 2/3). It needs the liquid-only admission of acetic acid. Its reaction heat is small, about -4 kJ/mol from liquid formation enthalpies, so the non-isothermal effect will show mainly through a jacket temperature step. A strongly exothermic textbook system would need other new species.
- **F5-T2 delivered on the Claude side (30 October 2026).** Acetic acid goes in `core/catalogo.REGISTRO_SOLO_LIQUIDO`, kept apart from `REGISTRO`. The published catalog, `especies_disponibles()` and every fingerprint are therefore unchanged. A comparison with HEAD showed identical package fingerprints for four VLE systems and an identical published catalog. Its pure data are sourced beside the entry: Wagner (Poling) Psat, Poling cp, DIPPR v_L 5.763e-5 m3/mol, and UNIFAC CH3 + COOH in both tables. The liquid formation data come from `chemicals.reaction`: Hfl -483.58 kJ/mol (ATcT; CRC -484.3) and S0l 159.8 J/(mol K). `catalogo.faltantes(..., solo_liquido=False)` reports the new code `ESPECIE_SOLO_LIQUIDO` (dato `fase_vapor`). So `Sistema(pid, cas)` rejects acetic acid in every VLE package, and so do the common layer (`capa._sistema`), flashes, L-V paths and diagrams. `Sistema(pid, cas, solo_liquido=True)` admits it and raises `ESPECIE_SOLO_LIQUIDO` on every vapour evaluation: vapour mu, vapour phase, `lv_ln_k`, vapour stability trial, `P_saturacion`, TP/PH/UP/TV/UV and seeded flashes, and `lv_continuation.trace`. Its fingerprint adds `soloLiquido: true`. In `core/reactions/chemical_equilibrium.py`, `IdealGasPhase` and a vapour `PackagePhase` reject the species. On a liquid-only system the stability report runs only liquid trials and returns `vapour_assessed: false`, so stability against vapour formation is not certified. Tests: `tests/test_liquid_only_admission.py`, 11 pass, with the esterification extent 0.672 +- 0.002 and certified. `tests/test_chemical_equilibrium.py` now uses the registered species without in-process patching; it passes 15 tests and `test_reaction_relations` passes 7. Catalog-dependent suites, all passing: `test_v2_compatibility` 7, `test_catalog_species_domains` 5, `test_phase_discovery` 9, `test_common_phase_window` 4, `test_three_liquid_ternary` 9, `test_transient_phase_window` 2, `test_unifac_performance_paths` 6, `validar_motor_catalogo` 126, `validar_capa_termodinamica` 179, `validar_capa_unifac` 82, `validar_motor_unifac` 136, `validar_motor_n_componentes` 82 and `validar_motor_ensamblaje_v2` 114.
- **F5-T2 independent review (30 October 2026, read-only for code). Verdict after the re-check of 31 October 2026: approve. R1 and R2 are repaired and verified (see the re-check below); R3 remains a recorded, non-blocking validity limit.** Scope: the uncommitted liquid-only admission in `core/catalogo.py`, `core/mezclas.py`, `core/mezclas_datos.py`, `core/reactions/chemical_equilibrium.py`, `tests/test_chemical_equilibrium.py` and `tests/test_liquid_only_admission.py`, together with `core/termodinamica/derived_bridge.py` and `tests/test_derived_thermo_bridge.py`. What holds:
  - *Vapour paths.* Every vapour entry point of `Sistema` calls `_exigir_vapor`: `mu('vapor')`, `fase('vapor')` (through `mu`), `lv_ln_k`, the vapour trial of `prueba_estabilidad`, `P_saturacion`, `T_saturacion` (through `P_saturacion`), `equilibrio_TP`/`_isobaro` (PH, UP)/`_TV`/`_UV` and `flash_rapido`. `_puro_TP`, `_mesetas`, `_meseta_general` and `_newton` are reached only after one of those guards. Outside `Sistema`, the only direct users of `g_ig0`/`h_ig` are `IdealGasPhase`, which rejects the species, and `derived_bridge`, which drops the gas fields and guards `_phase_props('vapor')`. No backend route passes `solo_liquido`. Every published path iterates `especies_disponibles()`, which excludes acetic acid, and `capa._sistema` rejects it. The data object `mezclas_datos.especie('64-19-7')` (its `Psat`, `g_ig0`) is now constructible directly, but no route calls it for this species.
  - *Catalog and fingerprints.* `git archive HEAD core backend` was compared with the working tree. The digests are identical for `catalogo_publico()`, `capacidades_mezcla()`, `huella_datos_puros` of the 8 species, `faltantes` of the 8 species in every package, and `Sistema.huella()` of all 552 systems (every package, every 1-, 2- and 3-species subset). The frontend strict reader is therefore unaffected.
  - *Data, checked independently (chemicals 1.5.2, thermo 0.6.1).*
    - `Hfl` −483.58 kJ/mol (ATcT_L; CRC −484.3) and `S0l` 159.8 J/(mol K) (CRC) are confirmed.
    - Liquid reaction values: ΔH° −4515 J/mol, ΔS° 7.20 J/(mol K), ΔG° −6661.7 J/mol, K = 14.69. The code's ln K of 2.68729 equals −ΔG°/RT exactly.
    - Psat: the package gives 2076 Pa at 298.15 K. The NIST Antoine value (Ambrose and Ghiassee) is 2114 Pa (−1.8 %); `thermo` gives 2082 Pa. At 391.1 K the package gives 101516 Pa against 101609 Pa.
    - v_L gives ρ = 1042.0 kg/m3, against CRC 1044.6 (−0.25 %).
  - *Conversion, checked independently.* An own Brent solve of Σν(ln x + ln γ) = ln K used `thermo`'s Dortmund UNIFAC with DDBST groups: EtOH CH3/CH2/OH(P); AcOH CH3/COOH (42); EtOAc CH3/CH2/CH3COO (21); water H2O. It gives ξ = 0.6718, against the code's 0.67179. The ideal-liquid conversion is 0.7931.
  - *Stability report.* `vapour_assessed: false` is returned by both `PackagePhase.stability` and `derived_bridge`, and `certified` never depends on stability. Note, not a fix: the `stable` key still reads `true` when vapour was not assessed. Consumers must read `stable` together with `vapour_assessed`, never alone.
  - *Tests.* `test_liquid_only_admission`, `test_chemical_equilibrium` and `test_derived_thermo_bridge` pass 45 together. `test_v2_compatibility` + `test_reaction_relations`: 14 pass together (7 + 7, 95 min).
  
  Findings:
  - **(R1, required) The gas anchor still uses the invalid vapour route for liquid-only species, and nothing reports it.** `PackagePhase(Sistema(..., solo_liquido=True), 'liquido')` defaults to `anchor='gas'`, as does `derived_bridge.reaction_enthalpy`. Reproduce it with the esterification at 298.15 K and 1 bar, n0 = (1, 1, 0, 0):
    - `solve_extents` gives ξ = 0.868 with `certified: true`. The only warning is `stable: false`: a water-rich liquid split (tm −0.12) at that wrong composition.
    - `reaction_enthalpy(..., anchor='gas', liquid_only=True)` gives ΔH −13.33 kJ/mol, against the tabulated −4.515 kJ/mol. Acetic acid's h comes out as −474.65 kJ/mol instead of −483.58 kJ/mol.
    
    The fix: when any species of the system is in `REGISTRO_SOLO_LIQUIDO`, `PackagePhase` should reject `anchor='gas'` with a coded error (for example `LIQUID_ANCHOR_REQUIRED`). The bridge inherits that rejection through `_shift`. Add a test for each entry point.
  - **(R2, minor) The liquid-anchor provenance names the wrong source.** With `anchor='liquid'`, `provenance['formation']` and the bridge's `formation` still report the ideal-gas methods and `state: 'ideal gas, 298.15 K, 1 bar'`. The values actually come from `Hfl`/`S0l` (ATCT_L/CRC, pure liquid). The fix is to report the liquid methods and state.
  - **(R3, record, non-blocking) Liquid caloric properties away from T0 still come from the monomer ideal gas through Psat.** The liquid anchor fixes g, h and s only at T0. The package's liquid cp for acetic acid is 120.3, 112.1 and 107.5 J/(mol K) at 298, 330 and 350 K. VDI tabular values (via `thermo`) are 120.2, 128.8 and 134.2, so the deviation grows to −13 % and −20 %, and the package's cp falls with T while the real cp rises. For comparison, ethanol under the same route gives 119.6 at 350 K, against about 137. This leaves the isothermal 298.15 K conversion unchanged. It does bias the Demo 0.1 jacket-step energy balance and K(T) above T0. Record it next to F5-C2 and F5-C3 as a validity range, or replace it with a tabulated liquid cp before the non-isothermal demo is certified.
  - **R3 resolved (Claude, 31 October 2026; uncommitted, review pending).** `core/mezclas_datos.py` gives every liquid-only species a tabulated liquid cp. For acetic acid this is Perry's 8th ed. table 2-153 (DIPPR equation 100, `chemicals` `Cp_data_Perry_Table_153_100`), valid from 289.81 to 391.05 K; this range now bounds the system. The pure liquid is one Gibbs function anchored to the unchanged Psat route at (298.15 K, 1 bar): h_L = h*_L(T0, P0) + ∫cp_L dT + v_L(P − P0), s_L = s*_L(T0, P0) + ∫cp_L/T dT, g_L = h_L − T s_L. Formation anchors, the −4515 J/mol at 298.15 K and the isothermal conversion are therefore unchanged. cp_L is 123.9, 131.6 and 137.4 J/(mol K) at 298, 330 and 350 K, against 120.3, 112.1 and 107.5 on the old route; it is within 1 % of CRC at 298.15 K and within 5 % of the first VDI point at 391.15 K. Gibbs-Helmholtz and dg/dP = v_L hold numerically. The optional fields are left out of the fingerprint while absent, so vapour-liquid fingerprints are unchanged. The `test_derived_package_reactor` peak moves from 337.1757 to 337.5072 K and still follows the independent reference within 1e-5 K. Tests: `tests/test_liquid_only_admission.py` adds `LiquidHeatCapacity` (2 tests); 16 tests pass there, and 48 across the thermodynamic, equilibrium and bridge suites.

The bridge's package reference (`anchor=None`) is allowed in `phase_caloric` and the other caloric calls. It applies no formation shift, so it uses no gas formation data, but its h_L carries the R3 caloric route.

The reaction heat enters only through the package inventory: T peaks at 337.1757 K at about 8 s, resolved on a 1 s grid over the first 60 s and checked against the same independent reference (`test_reaction_heat_peak_resolved_on_the_first_minute`, within 1e-5 K). *Corrected 31 October 2026 (review RV-1):* an earlier version of this line gave 331.73 K, which is only the 50 s sample of the 61-point, 3000 s grid. The F5-C3 row reports 0.038 (tolerance 1e-3) against the certified equilibrium (checked against `minimise_gibbs`), so these rate constants are not in detailed balance with K(T); Delta_r H -2.76 kJ/mol at 330 K. The compiled flash path rejects the liquid-only volume with `MOTOR_ENS_ESPECIE_SOLO_LIQUIDO`; VLE models still reject acetic acid. R3 (acetic acid liquid cp) still limits certification above 298 K.
