**F9-R6 measurement (Claude, 23 October 2026; no production change).** `python tools/unifac_benchmark/run_benchmark.py` times 29 cases in separate processes with a 300 s cap, 3 repeats, environment and source digests recorded (output in ignored `.tmp/claude-r6-benchmark/benchmark.json`). It covers original UNIFAC with 2–8 species and Dortmund with 2/3/5/8 species (uniform feed, 350 K, 101325 Pa), six phase regimes, ethanol and water traces of 1e-6/1e-12/1e-300, and seeded heating trajectories of 10 and 40 samples. Every published state passed the R2 validity rule; rejections were the known UV liquid-fills-volume case. `--profile <case>` writes a cProfile breakdown.

| Closure | Measured (cold, single run) | Cause from the profile |
|---|---|---|
| Binary TP / PH / seeded step | 0.003–0.05 s / 0.02–0.30 s / 0.002–0.07 s | Grid stability search; no hot spot. |
| Ternary TP / PH | 0.4–6.5 s / 2.6–10.6 s; ternary LLE TP 6.5 s | 83–85 % in the three-species liquid stability test: a 1,891-point grid, then 12 Nelder-Mead refinements from the 12 lowest points (often the same basin) with about 1,000 scalar UNIFAC evaluations each. |
| Ternary seeded dynamic step | 0.74 s per sample, no fallback (k = 5: 0.008 s) | The same stability test runs after every Newton step. |
| 4–8 species TP / PH | 0.36–2.0 s / 0.08–0.21 s | The general stability method is about ten times cheaper than the ternary path. |
| UV | Binary vapor/VLE 0.7–1.2 s; 4–8 species 5–50 s; ternary vapor at 350 K does not finish in 300 s (benchmark) or 890 s (direct probe, TP 0.52 s and PH 10.6 s for the same state) | Nested Brent searches over `equilibrio_TV` with an inner Brent: about 176 TP closures for k = 5. |

**Budgets accepted by the user on 23 October 2026** as R6 acceptance thresholds for one closure on this machine: seeded dynamic step median ≤ 0.05 s; full TP ≤ 1 s; PH ≤ 2 s; UV ≤ 5 s; all up to eight species and any admitted trace. An optimization is accepted only when the benchmark meets them with unchanged certification tolerances and every published state valid. Before the optimization below, the ternary TP/PH/step, the ternary LLE and every UV with four or more species exceeded them, and the ternary UV case was a non-terminating closure in practice. Volume scaling in networks is not measured in this step.

**F9-R6 optimization (Claude, 23 October 2026; `core/mezclas.py`, `TOL_TM`/`TOL_MU` unchanged).** (1) Ternary stability: the same 60-lattice and its 12 lowest seeds, each refined by a descent on Michelsen's tm* in log amounts (Newton direction of the stationarity conditions when it descends, otherwise the successive-substitution direction; Armijo steps; at most 0.05 change in any mole fraction per step), with Nelder-Mead kept as the per-seed fallback; seeds reaching an already polished minimum reuse it, so the published trial list (`estabilidad.pruebas`) keeps one entry per seed. (2) UV: `_uv_acotado` brackets T between the ideal-vapor and liquid energy bounds, takes loose seeded TV closures by regula falsi and tries the existing Newton UV from each; it returns only a converged, fully stability-tested Newton state, otherwise the nested search runs unchanged. Evidence (`.tmp/claude-r6-optimize/`): 432 ternary references, six systems, four temperatures and traces to 1e-300, compared seed by seed with the previous Nelder-Mead refinement: same trial counts and certification decisions, minimum tm equal to 1e-14, no seed higher except one unstable feed reference where Nelder-Mead had left its seed's basin, and lower values at 1e-300 traces where Nelder-Mead stalled; 10 of 5,184 seeds used the fallback; stability test 0.7 s to 0.015-0.03 s. The F0 dense-search cross-check on its 468 states: 0 misses, package minimum changed by at most 9e-15. Benchmark rerun: every case within the budgets: seeded step median at most 0.017 s (ternary 0.74 s before), TP at most 0.40 s (cold first call; ternary LLE 6.5 s before), PH at most 0.23 s, UV at most 2.2 s (ternary vapor 0.58 s, previously not finishing; eight species 0.7-2.2 s), every published state valid and the same liquid-fills-volume rejections. `tests/test_unifac_performance_paths.py` keeps the per-seed equivalence and the seeded UV result. Network volume scaling remains unmeasured.

**F9-R7 compiler repairs and v2 step (b) (Codex, 24 October 2026; ready for review).** Shared `core/compilador/admission.py` now rejects incompatible datum dimensions by consuming role, non-finite/empty data, nonpositive horizons, negative/empty inventories, empty network/species sets, duplicate or invalid CAS, missing extraction for any possible donor, inconsistent references and physical assumptions, and out-of-scope topology/package/study settings. Located diagnostics reach both common HTTP endpoints as structured 422 responses. Unit-equivalent inputs preserve the physical RHS; a shared datum ID is checked for every role. The original compiler fails 16 R7 admission assertions and four additional physical-assumption assertions in the new regressions. Constant-density, material-source and phase assumptions are also checked rather than dropped. No thermodynamics files were edited.

Step (b) reuses the same admission path and existing certified package TP/TV/UV/UP operations. Prescribed-pressure initial U now works for mixtures and water; missing, incompatible and unsupported initialization have explicit diagnostic states. The archive regression admits and initializes **19 of 25 requests** and rejects **six** for their expected reasons, including the open-boundary draft; initial quantities agree within relative 1e-7 and the 25-request/24-response manifest hash is unchanged. The simulated missing-UNIFAC-assignment case uses the archive generator's scoped injection; `u2-mono` is compatible initially and its later stop remains a runtime test. `datosPuros` changes the shared package fingerprint and initial state. Complete results, certification and adapter parity are not claimed. Stop after (b), before (c); no route switch or deletion.

**R7 v2 boundary-work diagnosis (behavior unchanged).** A fresh run of the audit's two isobaric benzene/toluene volumes at extensive scale 1e-6 gives work 0.000399013462436686 J versus the independent identity P(V - V0) = 0.000387957150596409 J: **2.8498796% error**. The work residual/tolerance is 11.0136, numerical status is `resuelto`, but the physical certificate is already `no_cumple` with `MOTOR_ENS_CERTIFICACION_NO_CUMPLE`, first failure 1 and last certified sample 0. Thus the numerical status must not be presented as physical validity. Changing only that test request's absolute tolerances by the same 1e-6 scale reduces relative work error to 5.36e-12 and residual/tolerance to 5.29e-6; second-law certification stays `no_evaluable` because boundary temperature is missing. This identifies sensitivity to unscaled auxiliary absolute tolerance, not a validated production fix. Preserve the failing identity as a step-(c) work/certification control before changing native behavior.

**F9-R7 adverse-case audit (Claude, 24 October 2026; read-only, reproducers in ignored `.tmp/claude-r7-audit/`).** One revision-2 request (two open isobaric `mezcla-ideal` benzene–toluene volumes, A heated with 100 W and drained into adiabatic B at 0.001 mol/s by homogeneous extraction, 100 s) is solved by the network assembly (`resolver_v2`) and by the common compiler (`desde_ensamblaje_v2` → `core.compilador.resolver`) with 37 variants each (`cases.py`, `compare.py`); the balance generator is exercised separately with closed-form cascades (`probe_balances.py`). References are the permuted, scaled or unperturbed run, Bateman's solution or hand-derived invariants, never the engine's own residuals.

- **Passed.** Component order, volume list order and volume ids permute exactly (common: largest difference 6e-15, identical event time; v2: 2.5e-10). Extensive scaling by 1e3 and 1e-6 keeps T, P, fractions and the phase-appearance time (55.8007277 s) within 5e-11 s in the common path. Two output samples reproduce the event and the final state. With Q = 0 the homogeneously drained isobaric donor keeps T = 330 K (5.7e-14 K) and n = n0 − F t (1.1e-16); with F = 0 the receiver does not drift (1.1e-13); an exact zero and a 1e-12 trace of benzene in B agree to 1.4e-14 and the zero stays exactly zero. The balance generator permutes and scales exactly, matches Bateman to 3e-12, stops constant draining at the exhaustion time (t = 1 s, `agotamiento`) and rejects every missing, non-finite, duplicated, empty, outside-to-outside, zero/negative-horizon and fewer-than-two-sample input with `ErrorModelo`/`NoSoportado`. v2 rejects all 22 malformed requests with a coded diagnostic and never returns `resuelto`.
- **High, common compiler (Codex, `core/compilador/compilador.py` `_Dato`).** Data are converted with the unit's factor and offset but the dimension is never checked against the role. `repro_units.py`: a heat rate declared as `0 degC` is applied as 273.15 W and A ends at 348.43 K instead of 330 K; a molar flow declared in `kg/s`, or in `K` (`cases.py wrong_unit_flow`), is read as mol/s. All are published `resuelto`; v2 answers `MOTOR_ENS_UNIDAD`.
- **High, thermodynamics (Claude, `core/mezclas.py` `equilibrio_isobaro`).** For a single present species with a target H below the saturated-liquid value hL(Ts) by less than about 1e-9 |hL|, the liquid-side bracket ends at Ts(1 − 1e-9), so the root between that point and Ts is excluded and `FueraDeAlcance` is raised (`repro_pure_bubble_edge.py`: toluene and benzene at 101325 Pa, H − hL = −2.4e-5 J). The common compiler's event probes land in that band, so heating pure liquid toluene to boiling, or the binary request with benzene at zero in every volume, aborts (`cases.py pure_toluene`, `zero_species_everywhere`); v2 solves both (appearance at 83.6044 s, plateau at 383.790101 K). **Fixed (user approval, 24 October 2026; `TOL_TM`, `TOL_MU` and the PH tolerance unchanged, no species or case branch):** the one-phase bracket now ends at Ts itself, evaluated with the phase of its side (`_puro_TP` with the known side is well defined there), on both the liquid and the vapor side. Independent reference for the rerun network cases: with homogeneous extraction n dh/dt = Q, so h(t) = h0 + (Q/F) ln(n0/(n0 − F t)) with h0, hL(Ts), hV(Ts) from pure-component TP evaluations: bubble at 83.604400444 s, plateau at Ts = 383.790100921 K and vapor fraction 0.052126623960 at 100 s by the lever rule. The common path now solves `pure_toluene` and `zero_species_everywhere` (`resuelto`, about 8 s each): appearance at 83.6044004569 s (1.3e-8 s from the reference), T at 100 s equal to Ts to 1e-9 K, vapor fraction 0.0521266239206 (4e-11 from the lever rule), max |H/n − h_ref| 1.9e-5 J/mol, n_A = n0 − F t to 1e-16 (`check_pure_after.py`). v2 agrees for pure toluene (83.6044004555 s); its run with the declared zero species places the event 3.5e-5 s late (83.6044351 s), a v2-only accuracy note. Regression: `python tests/test_pure_isobaric_bracket.py` (3 tests, about 2 s): one-phase PH and UP states 1e-9, 1e-11 and 1e-13 relative beyond each plateau end for ideal toluene and benzene, NRTL ethanol and Dortmund water against T = Ts + dy/c (c from TP central differences), the lever rule on the toluene plateau, and a binary with an exact-zero species equal to the pure system. **Negative control:** against the pre-fix module (`.tmp/claude-r7-audit/negative_control.py`) it gives 17 errors. Suites passing after the fix: pure bracket 3; transient phase window 2; trace phase search 3; invariant polish 5; near-pure isobaric flash 6; phase discovery 9; catalog domains 5; thermodynamic derivation 11; thermodynamic events 6; isobaric bubble edge 2; bubble-edge H noise 3; common phase window 4; retired v1 4; thermodynamics; thermodynamic layer 179; water 161; engine thermodynamics 585; H3 3,729; diagrams 78; catalog 126; vertical slice 71; balances 30; assembly v2 114; n-components 82; consolidation v2 128; engine UNIFAC 136 (1,034 s); UNIFAC layer 82 (4,821 s). The R6 benchmark keeps every budget (seeded step at most 0.0139 s per sample, PH at most 0.21 s, UV at most 1.50 s; the one cold TP of 1.25 s for original UNIFAC k = 4 was first-call noise: 0.31 s when rerun alone, warm median 0.03 s). The validators rewrote `capa-ebullicion-agua.json`, `capa-calentamiento-benceno-tolueno.json` and `corte-vertical-benceno-tolueno-resultados.json`; the pre-fix module writes byte-identical files, so those differences are not caused by this fix (the working-tree examples predate the current code: event shifts up to 4.5e-7 s); all three were restored to their previous content.
- **Medium, common path (Codex).** Non-structured failures: zero or negative horizon reaches scipy (`max_step must be positive`, `compilador.py` `resolver` does not validate tf, unlike the balance-generator route); an empty `componenteIds` or no volumes raises `ValueError` from `max()`; negative initial amounts, an empty volume and P = 0 surface as thermodynamic `FueraDeAlcance`; `nan`, `inf` and non-numeric data as `ErrorExpresion`. `/modelo/resolver` catches only `NoSoportado`/`CapaNoSoportada`, so `repro_http.py` gets HTTP 500 for four of them (a missing reference correctly gets 422). Nothing invalid is published.
- **Medium, common path (Codex).** An undeclared extraction rule defaults to `mezcla_homogenea` (`compilador.py` line 263): a negative flow on stream A→B with only A's rule is solved as homogeneous extraction from B (B stays at 300 K), an unrecorded closure; v2 answers `MOTOR_ENS_EXTRACCION`. Two components with the same CAS are accepted and solved (benzene twice boils at 353.24 K); v2 answers `MOTOR_ENS_IDENTIDAD_DUPLICADA`.
- **Medium, v2 network (being migrated).** At scale 1e-6 the auxiliary boundary-work integral W of A is 2.85 % off the unscaled run (absolute energy `atol` 1e-6 J against W ≈ 4e-4 J); v2's own work residual is 11 against tolerance 1 and its certification says `no_cumple`, yet `estado` is `resuelto`. All states (n, U, H, T, V, S) stay within 8e-12. The common path does not publish W (already a listed parity gap).
- **Low.** The common path accepts a self-loop stream A→A as a no-op (identical to F = 0); v2 rejects it as `MOTOR_ENS_TOPOLOGIA`. Absolute tolerances are not scaled with the inventory: at 1e-9 kg the balance generator differs by 3.9e-5 relative from the scaled reference while staying within the declared `atol` (error against Bateman 4e-16 kg). The common compiler takes 82–141 s per case against 20–52 s for v2 on this ideal binary.

R7 stays open: findings in Codex's files are handed to Codex on the board; the `core/mezclas.py` pure-component bracket is fixed; further R7 coverage (more species, rigid volumes, UNIFAC networks, multiple solutions) follows once these are resolved.

**Independent review of the F9-R7 compiler admission repairs (Claude, 24 October 2026; read-only, evidence in ignored `.tmp/claude-r7-audit/`).** Verdict: **accepted for the audited malformed-request scope.** All 36 common-path cases of the audit battery rerun on the current tree (`cases.py`, `compare.py`): every malformed request is now a structured rejection (`AdmissionError`/`ErrorModelo`), including dimension conflicts by consuming role (`0 degC` heat, molar flow in `kg/s` or `K`), non-finite and non-numeric data, nonpositive horizons, negative or empty inventories, zero pressure, duplicate CAS, self-loops, empty species lists, no volumes and a reversible flow without the receiver's extraction rule; `repro_units.py` and `repro_http.py` now get HTTP 422 with located diagnostics instead of `resuelto` or HTTP 500. The accepted cases are unchanged: the base trajectory and event are bit-identical to the pre-repair run, permutations and scalings keep the same differences (at most 6e-15 and 4.4e-12) and `zero_species_everywhere` matches pure toluene within 4.4e-12. Independent negative control: the same cases in an isolated copy with the `HEAD` compiler and API (no `admission.py`) return `resuelto` for the wrong unit, duplicate CAS, undeclared reversal extraction and self-loop, and the unstructured exception for a zero horizon. `tests/test_common_admission.py` (7) and `tests/test_v2_initialization.py` (6) pass. **New finding, medium (Codex):** time-dependent data are range-checked only at t = 0 (`admission.py` `datum`, `value(0)`). A prescribed pressure `101325 - 2026.5*t` Pa is admitted, reaches 2234 Pa mid-run and raises `FueraDeAlcance` with no published prefix; `/modelo/resolver` answers HTTP 500, while v2 rejects the same request at admission with `MOTOR_ENS_FUERA_DE_MAGNITUD` (`probe_time_data.py`, `probe_pressure_http.py`). Handled correctly: a heat rate singular at t = 50 s stops `parcial` at 50 s with the certified prefix, and a flow that reverses mid-run is rejected for the missing receiver extraction. The v2 boundary-work drift at 1e-6 scale stays the step-(c) control.

**Independent review of v2 step (b) admission and initialization (Claude, 24 October 2026; read-only, `.tmp/claude-v2b-review/review_initialization.py`).** Verdict: **accepted; step (c) may start.** Each of the 25 archived revision-2 requests was judged by the native v2 validator itself (`validar_v2`) and by the common path (`desde_ensamblaje_v2` → `compilar(...).inicial()`), and every admitted initial state was compared with the archived response's t = 0 sample written by the native solver. All 20 requests admitted natively are admitted by the common path, and their initial n, T, P, V, U and H agree within 1.8e-14 relative (worst 1.43e-13, the low-pressure water contact case; three cases are bit-identical). The four native rejections give the same native codes (`MOTOR_ENS_Q_PENDIENTE` with `MOTOR_ENS_REFERENCIAS_INCOMPATIBLES`, `MOTOR_ENS_PARAMETRO_AUSENTE`, `MOTOR_ENS_Q_PENDIENTE`, `MOTOR_ENS_EXTRACCION`). The simulated missing-UNIFAC-assignment archive is admitted by both without its generator's injection, consistent with Codex's scoped injection. **Low, for step (d) response compatibility:** the open-boundary draft is rejected on both sides, but the common path rejects it in the strict reader (`ErrorModelo` on `frontera.tipo` `rigida_con_presion`) without a native code, whereas v2 reports five codes (`MOTOR_ENS_DONANTE_PENDIENTE`, `FRONTERA`, `INICIAL`, `MATERIA`, `MODELO`); the adapter must map that rejection when it reproduces v2 validation responses. The open medium finding on time-dependent data (above) stays with Codex.

**F9-R7 extension: rigid volumes, three and five species, UNIFAC (Claude, 24 October 2026; read-only, `.tmp/claude-r7-ext/`).** Fourteen requests per route (`cases_ext.py`, `compare_ext.py`): two rigid ideal benzene–toluene volumes (A heated 50 W, A–B contact 0.5 W/K); one isobaric original-UNIFAC water/ethanol/acetone volume heated 100 W; an isobaric ideal five-species network with species absent from B. **Passed:** v2 conserves U_A + U_B = U0 + Q t to 1.5e-11 J and the UNIFAC volume H = H0 + Q t to 4e-12 J (common 1.7e-8 J); cyclic and two-species permutations of the UNIFAC ternary, reversed five-species order, volume order and ids and scaling by 1e3 agree within 3e-14 (common) and 1.5e-11 (v2), with event shifts at most 1.4e-9 s; species absent from B stay exactly zero; v2 and the common path agree on the same requests within 4e-11 and 6e-10 s in the event time (appearance at 20.1549291 s and 34.7900874 s). At scale 1e-4 (rigid) and 1e-5 (five species) v2 differs by up to 8.4e-7 in P and 4.8e-6 in the auxiliary W, consistent with its unscaled absolute tolerances (already recorded). **High, thermodynamics (Claude, `core/mezclas.py` `equilibrio_TV`):** every rigid case aborts in the common path while v2 solves it. The common path's cold certification of published samples (`capa.cierre` → `equilibrio_UV`) runs the nested T search with the loose TV search (`rtol=1e-7` in ln P). Near a bubble point V(P) is steep (state B: 0.2/0.8 benzene/toluene, 2 L, 313.36 K, about 40 Pa below its bubble pressure; d ln V / d ln P of order 10^2), so the Brent tolerance leaves a volume error above the acceptance threshold `max(1e-9, 100 rtol) V`; the fallback takes it for an invariant coexistence, finds none and raises `FueraDeAlcance("no_convergente")`. `repro_rigid_uv.py` reproduces it from v2's published states: cold UV fails at t = 100 and 200 s, while TV at v2's temperature with the default tolerance gives P = 11256.3 Pa and reproduces U within 1e-9 J (`diag_rigid_uv.py`: the same TV with `rtol=1e-7` fails, with `1e-15` it converges). **Fixed (user approval, 24 October 2026; `TOL_TM`, `TOL_MU` and every closure tolerance unchanged, no species or case branch):** when the loose search leaves a volume error above its threshold, `equilibrio_TV` now narrows the bracket the search already enclosed (ln P ± 2 rtol (1 + |ln P|), checked for a sign change) with a tight Brent search before testing for an invariant coexistence; genuine jumps still reach the coexistence test. Regression: `python tests/test_steep_tv_bracket.py` (about 10 s) against Raoult's law with the Poynting factor and Rachford–Rice written in the test: two feeds, three temperatures, P from 1e-2 to 1e-4 below the bubble pressure; TV with `rtol` 1e-7 and 1e-15 returns the reference P, the vapor amount matches within 1e-7 and cold UV returns T within 1e-7 K. **Negative control:** 14 errors against the pre-fix module. The six `rig_*` cases now solve in the common path (12–14 s): U_A + U_B = U0 + Q t within 1.5e-11 J, component/volume permutations within 1.3e-13, scaling by 1e3 within 1.2e-8, agreement with v2 within 3.7e-9; at scale 1e-4 both routes differ by 6–8e-7 in P, consistent with the unscaled absolute tolerances. Suites passing after the fix: steep TV 1; pure bracket 3; transient window 2; trace search 3; invariant polish 5; near-pure flash 6; phase discovery 9; catalog domains 5; thermodynamic derivation 11; thermodynamic events 6; isobaric bubble edge 2; bubble-edge noise 3; UNIFAC performance paths 6; common phase window 4; retired v1 4; common admission 8; v2 initialization 6; thermodynamics; thermodynamic layer 179; water 161; engine thermodynamics 585; diagrams 78; catalog 126; balances 30; H3 3,729; assembly v2 114; n-components 82; consolidation v2 128; engine UNIFAC 136; UNIFAC layer 82. `validar_corte_vertical.py` gives 69 of 71 with or without this fix: its two rejection checks look for the words `constitutivas` and `supuesto`, which Codex's new admission messages no longer contain (Codex is editing `admission.py` under its step-(c) claim). The R6 benchmark keeps every budget (TP at most 0.32 s, PH 0.19 s, UV 1.52 s, seeded step 0.014 s per sample, every state valid). Validator rewrites of three `docs/ejemplos/modelo-comun/` files are byte-identical to the pre-fix outputs and were restored.

**F9-R7 extension: UNIFAC networks with streams and multiple solutions (Claude, 24-29 October 2026; read-only, `.tmp/claude-r7-final/`).** Requests from `cases_final.py`, compared by `compare_final.py <case>` against independent references (convex-hull G minimum, H0 + Q t, F z t inventories, finite-difference feed enthalpy). Multiple-solution cases: closed isobaric volumes heated at constant Q: water/1-butanol 0.7/0.3 from 355 K through L-L, an L-L-V plateau and L-V with NRTL-LLE (`ms_nrtl_wb`) and Dortmund UNIFAC (`ms_dort_wb`), and water/ethanol 0.12/0.88 near the azeotrope from 345 K, L to L-V, with NRTL ChemSep (`ms_nrtl_ew`). Both routes publish the global G minimum at every sample (|G_pub - G_hull| at most 1.7e-6 J/mol), conserve energy within 1.8e-11 J, place appearance/disappearance events within 1.1e-5 s of the hull references, give the same final state and events with 2 and 11 samples (1.2e-8 s) and after a restart at 100 s (2.1e-7 s), and agree with each other within 1.4e-9. Networks, isobaric at 101325 Pa: a two-volume Dortmund-UNIFAC water/1-butanol/acetone network, feed to a decanter and phase extraction to B (`n2`), and a three-volume original-UNIFAC water/1-butanol/ethanol/acetone network with an exterior feed (`n3`): species inventories within 2.2e-16 mol, energy within 1.5e-11 J, absent species exactly zero, component and volume permutations within 9e-10, common-native agreement within 1.0e-9 and 1.4e-11 s in the event time. **Findings, native v2 only:** at scale 1e3 `n2` stops `parcial` at the located appearance event (t = 63.156 s, `MOTOR_ENS_MUESTRA_NO_CERTIFICADA`) while the common path certifies the same event within 6.6e-12 s; `n3` at scale 1e3 differs by 1.35e-6 (common) and 2.3e-6 (cross-route) relative in a trace acetone amount, consistent with the unscaled absolute tolerances already recorded. The phase-selection check of `n2` (receiver gain against each extracted liquid) is inconclusive at the published sample density (trapezoid errors 0.096 and 0.110 mol), so it is not counted as evidence either way. No common-path or Claude-owned defect remains; R7 is closed and the native findings follow the native v2 retirement decision.

**F9-R7 horizon/stop repair and v2 step (c), Codex, updated 27 October 2026; ready for scoped review.** Whole-horizon interval admission now replaces the earlier 401-point screen. Every temporal expression must have a finite real enclosure and satisfy its role's sign constraint after SI conversion; role dimensions are checked separately. Recursive subdivision has finite limits and rejects unresolved expressions with structured `unbounded_time_data` / `MOTOR_ENS_EXPRESION`. The linear pressure reaches zero at 50 s; three new negative controls cover a narrow negative pressure dip between old grid points, a square root leaving its domain and an interior pole. All three fail against the previous implementation; a fourth control rejects a finite positive pressure whose required derivative is singular at t=0. All twelve repaired admission tests pass, including structured common HTTP responses and valid bounded nonlinear data. The latest fail-closed requirement intentionally changes declared heat singularities from runtime partial completion to admission rejection. Runtime thermodynamic probe/restart failures still retain the accepted prefix; initial failures remain located diagnostics. Reversal without the receiving donor's extraction remains rejected. Margin logic, seed restoration, stage-stop retries and tolerances are unchanged.

Step (c) reuses the existing auxiliary work/entropy accounting and one shared publication block (`backend/assembly_results.py`) while retaining the common compiler's physical RHS and RK45/BDF. Common results now carry signed stream/heat flows, mass, entropy, work, potentials, stable phase identities, catalog roles, package fingerprints, residuals and all four certification aspects. `procedencia.physicalCertification` and the full `equilibrium` evidence are independent of numerical `estado`; failed physical checks retain their first failure and last certified sample. Publication exceptions truncate aligned arrays/events; no prefix produces an empty failed result. The native 1e-6-scale work control still measures about 2.85% drift and explicitly reports `no_cumple`; no tolerances or native numerical algorithm were changed. Common controls use n = n0 - F t and W = P(V - V0), corrupted work, negative second-law generation and missing boundary temperature. HEAD lacks the new certificate/work outputs. See [the step-(c) contract evidence](dynamic-assembly.md#13-v2-compatibility-adapter-contract).

**Step-(c) supercritical control closed, Codex verification 27 October 2026.** Following Claude's water repair described below (`328845d`, `52e3bda`), the expected-failure marker is removed: all nine common Results tests pass, including supercritical event identity/first-law controls, small-scale native work drift, corrupted work, negative second-law generation and certified-prefix publication. The UNIFAC validator now reads structured phase identities and certified phase composition rather than event prose; its complete independent-reference battery passes all 82 checks. The vertical-slice validator passes all 71 checks with `--no-write`; no frozen archive was regenerated. Step (d) uses a separate compatibility adapter with common integration, existing wire validation/catalogs and shared initial-specification dispatch; production routes and the native solver remain present. All 25 archived requests were executed through the adapter and fresh native v2: 23 fresh-native comparisons pass, and 21 pass when unchanged historical series are also enforced. Strict findings remain for heteroazeotrope event timing (1.197 microseconds against 1 microsecond), near-zero decantador entropy, historical near-zero monophase work, and five-species historical heat drift (also present in current native v2). The reader passes 25 responses/7 adjuncts/197 volume states and rejects 19 corruptions. Full evidence, mappings, actual source digests, timings and commands are maintained in [the compatibility contract](dynamic-assembly.md#13-v2-compatibility-adapter-contract).

**Assigned step-(e) repairs (Codex, 28 October 2026; in review).** Claude accepted step (c) and R7 and assigned comparator repairs R1/R2/R3 only. The two independently zero quantities use their existing absolute floors; conductance heat uses the propagated operand bound; the heteroazeotrope uses the recorded integrator-free event reference at unchanged tolerance. All 25 stored request trajectories now pass the revised comparisons; this is evidence replay, not a new final-source 25-request solve. The native 1.1973224e-6 s event lateness remains an explicit finding. The seven physical adjuncts pass in a fresh 143.788 s run, and the unchanged wire/HTTP/unit controls pass. Verification: common admission **8 tests**, initialization **6** (including the unchanged 25-request/24-response manifest), common Results **8 passing controls and one expected-failure supercritical target**, solver stability **4**, stage-stop **4**, workspace handoffs **8**, retired v1 **4**, common phase windows **4**, native thermodynamic events **6**, native transient windows **2**, native consolidation **128 checks**, common results reader **34**, explicit compiler **27**, vertical slice **71 with --no-write**, and M1 U1 **12 independent-reference/refinement runs**. TypeScript, scoped ESLint, the live TypeScript certificate reader (two results/six corruptions), v2 contract reader and **1,391 physical-journal checks** pass. The common event-side mapping is checked against the independent narrow-window reference. Frozen A/B/C counts/hashes and the archive manifest remain unchanged. The Checklist registry/snapshot and board are updated in place. **Independent review of step (c) and the R7 horizon repair, and fix of the supercritical-water control (Claude, 24 October 2026; `.tmp/claude-stepc-review/`).** Horizon repair verified with Claude's own reproducers: the pressure `101325 - 2026.5*t` Pa is rejected at admission with `MOTOR_ENS_FUERA_DE_MAGNITUD` and HTTP 422, matching v2; the singular heat stops `parcial` at 50 s; a mid-run reversal without the receiver's extraction is rejected. Step (c) publication verified on the R7 base network (two isobaric ideal volumes with a stream): the common result carries all 22 native v2 series (including W, S, mass, signed stream and heat flows), with the largest difference from v2 5.4e-10 (W) and 4.6e-12 (S), and `physicalCertification` correctly `no_evaluable` without a boundary temperature. **Water control, root cause (Claude-owned `core/agua_iapws95.py`):** the common path's certified probes reach 16 mK below T_c, where `saturacion` used the `chemicals` saturation fits unrefined. Their Maxwell residual grows towards T_c (1.2e-9 at 16 mK, 1e-7 at 1 mK, 1.8e-6 at 10 microK), and the liquid term was scaled by rho' (dP/drho)_T', which vanishes at T_c, so `TOL_SATURACION_MAXWELL` rejected one-phase states even at 30 MPa, far from the saturation curve; TPD was 1.9e-15. v2 never certified a state that close to T_c, which is why only the common path stopped. **Fix (no tolerance changed):** (1) only where the fit fails its tolerance, `saturacion` refines rho' and rho'' with the Maxwell Newton already used by `coexistencia` (factored into `_newton_maxwell`), which reaches residuals of 1e-15 up to 1e-13 K from T_c; elsewhere the fit values are kept bit-identical (0 of 4,000 temperatures from 273.16 K to T_c − 0.05 K change); (2) the liquid pressure mismatch is scaled by max(Psat, rho' (dP/drho)_T'), as `_residuo_maxwell` already does, identical wherever the liquid modulus exceeds Psat; (3) at one ulp below T_c, where the two fit densities meet rho_c with (dP/drho)_T = 0 and `propiedades` divided by zero, the two-phase envelope is not resolvable in floating point and the state is treated as one fluid branch, as above T_c (`_campana`, used by `ramas` and the branch-naming sites). A first variant that always refined the fits changed TPD rounding at 280 K, 1000 Pa (TPD noise about 2e-6 Pa near the state, 2e-9 relative to P, against `TOL_ESTABILIDAD` 1e-9) and was rejected; that low-pressure TPD rounding sensitivity is a separate low finding to revisit. **Result:** the common path now completes the 30 MPa heating control with `cambio_denominacion` at 64.4210760097 s (v2 64.4210760097 s, agreement 1e-11 s; static EOS reference t = [h(T_c, P) − h(630 K, P)] m / Q = 64.421076025 s, 1.5e-8 s away), H = H0 + Q t within 4.7e-10 J and T monotone. Regression `python tests/test_water_saturation_near_critical.py` (3 tests, about 5 s): Maxwell conditions recomputed in the test, one-phase certification at 30 MPa from T_c − 0.1 K to T_c − 1e-5 K, and the heating control against the static reference. **Negative control:** 5 failures and 1 error against the pre-fix module. Suites passing after the fix (38 run): water layer 161, engine thermodynamics 585, H3 3,729, thermodynamic layer 179, retired v1 4, hydraulics 14, M1 U1 12 runs, vertical slice 71 (`--no-write`), assembly v2 114, consolidation v2 128, n-components 82, catalog 126, diagrams 78, balances 30, explicit compiler 27, engine UNIFAC 136, common admission 8, v2 initialization 6, phase windows, events, stage stops, margins and all R2–R9 thermodynamic regressions. **Two items for Codex:** `tests/test_common_network_results.py` now reports one unexpected success (the supercritical target passes; remove its expected-failure marker); `tests/validar_capa_unifac.py` raises `KeyError 'volumen.A.fase.fases).beta'` because step (c) changed the appearance-event description from `aparece vapor-1` to `aparece una fase (...)` and the validator reads the phase from its last word: publish the phase identity in a structured field or keep it in the text, and make the validator read the structured field. Validator rewrites of two `docs/ejemplos/modelo-comun/` files are byte-identical to the pre-fix outputs and were restored.

- [ ] Independent and experimental cross-checks per property/regime, separate from internal checks.
- [ ] Reproducibility of data, parameters, versions, seeds, references and units.
- [ ] Adverse cases: zeros, traces, permutations, scaling, missing parameters, domain, limiting phases and multiple solutions.
- [ ] Event location and control between samples consistent with the integrator; declare the remaining limits.
- [ ] Benchmarks scaling in species, phases, volumes and samples; explicit budgets.
- [ ] Safe initialization and data access under concurrency; caches tied to fingerprints and immutability.
- [ ] Long jobs, progress and real server-side cancellation when implemented; do not confuse it with cancelling the client's wait.
- [ ] Persistence/import/export and compatible migration of sheets; expired results not reused.
- [ ] Sensitivity and uncertainty of data/parameters; do not present rounding digits as experimental precision.
- [ ] Browser tests, accessibility and a compact UX with units on every relevant input.
- [ ] Reproducible packaging or deployment after closing the physical scope offered.

## F10. Comparison with the lab

**Closed by user decision (5 November 2026, `dec-f10-lab-rebuild`).** This phase compared the engine with the earlier `/laboratorio` module. The objective was always to remove that module and rebuild its capabilities from scratch through the engine, not to migrate or reconcile it; `/laboratorio` was deleted on 22 October 2026 (backup ref `refs/backups/pre-lab-deletion-2026-10-22`). There is nothing left to compare. Contrast with experimental or published data (for example UNIFAC against measured equilibria) is not a separate phase: it is an acceptance criterion inside each physical phase (F8b, F9) and in the definition of done below.

## 3. Definition of done for any phase

- [ ] Foundation and assumptions documented.
- [ ] Every user-facing example is built through the shared fundamental-law deduction tools, with persistent per-volume/per-family operation provenance. Final requests and reconstructed explanations do not satisfy this requirement. Audit and known migration gaps: [Model workspace specification](model-workspaces.md#model-derivation-standard-and-audit).
- [ ] Capability and domain published faithfully.
- [ ] Reproducible data and provenance.
- [ ] Positive and negative tests and independent references.
- [ ] Compatibility with earlier results, or changes explained numerically.
- [ ] Contract and real examples delivered by Claude.
- [ ] Reader, UI, units and results integrated and checked by Codex.
- [ ] Limits visible in the right place, without overloading the normal flow.
- [ ] State updated with a link to the evidence.

**Continuity from what has been delivered:** F1 integrated → F2 by milestones → F3; F4–F8 according to their dependencies and F9 cross-cutting. Claude continues its current task in the plan; Codex integrates the available delivery. F1 is not reopened, and reaction, high-pressure EOS and new physics solvers are not all opened at once because a report was written.


## F2.6 / F9 — property-driven rigid-vessel discovery (19 October 2026)

Implemented in the common thermodynamic service and the standard four workspaces. The new ordinary water–1-butanol model declares no result phase set, uses one original-UNIFAC package throughout and records material, first-law and constitutive-choice journals. Both common and native full heating runs complete and certify within their published scope. The template, actual solver fixtures and independent UNIFAC reference tests are specified in [the workspace record](model-workspaces.md#property-driven-phase-discovery-example-19-october-2026).

Three generic numerical findings are resolved: Newton trial amounts are checked against finite positive component inventories before exponentiation/normalization, with strict package T/P domains; phase-addition initial guesses include small amounts near a binodal; invariant-pressure polishing uses the actual discovery bracket rather than an unrelated narrower hard-coded window. Certification tolerances are unchanged. Nine new reference/adverse tests, existing UNIFAC 82, thermodynamics 585 and H3 3729 checks pass. This is a scoped F9-R2 improvement; broader candidate-domain and F9-R1 work incidents remain open. No NRTL coverage is extended. `/checklist` and its canonical export list the delivered capability and remaining limits.
